    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

共享零变量，Z3 无法跨 trace 比较。False-True 对最具辨别力 (non-empty 中 46.7% UNSAT)。最终对准确率无影响（仅覆盖 9/204 问题），第 6 个 negative constructive。核心诊断价值：变量命名不一致是 capability trap 的关键机制之一。\"}, \"exp-042-premise-formalization\": \"avg_filtered_t8\": 0.3}, \"notes\": \"Consistency filtering NEGATIVE: 过滤后约束数量骤降（T≥4 仅 1.8/题, T≥8 仅 0.3/题），MaxSAT 无法区分答案，退化为 SC。根因：Z3 变量命名不一致导致语义等价约束被视为不同（avg 13.2 unique constraints/problem），跨 trace \"created_at\": \"2026-05-18T17:18:09.052Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:53:05.656Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Z3 UNSAT core feedback refinement: closed-loop 方法用 Z3 solver 的 UNSAT core 指导 LLM 修正矛盾约束（最多 3 轮）。如果约束质量是瓶颈，solver 反馈应能提升 constraint discriminative power，使 Z3-feedback SICA > vanilla SICA。目标 recovery_rate > 0%（recovery_rate = Z3fb_delta / oracle_delta × 100%）。与之前 open-loop interventions（contrastive/cross-model/generational 全部失败）对比，closed-loop 利用 formal verification 信号可能是打破 capability trap 的关键。\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"notes\": \"Z3 feedback mechanism had zero real impact. Apparent +3.0pp (77.5% vs 74.5%) entirely due to code path confound: JSON serialization vs in-memory processing caused different dedup/scoring behavior. UNSAT trigger rate 0.9% (22/2448 traces), too low for feedback loop to meaningfully activate. All observed delta on discordant problems came from pipeline non-determinism, not Z3 refinement. Fourth negative constructive intervention after contrastive, cross-model, generational.\", \"metrics\": {\"z3fb_sica_accuracy\": 0.775, \"vanilla_sica_accuracy\": 0.745, \"sc_accuracy\": 0.75, \"z3fb_vs_vanilla_delta\": 0.03, \"unsat_traces\": 22, \"total_traces\": 2448, \"unsat_rate\": 0.009, \"unsat_resolve_rate\": 0.864, \"problems_flipped\": 0, \"real_impact\": 0}}, \"exp-028-qwen3-14b-thinking\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-18T14:02:12.168Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-18T18:06:48.306Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Qwen3-14B thinking mode 在 FOLIO 204 上 SICA vs SC：原生推理能力（内置 CoT）是否改变 capability trap？两种可能：(a) thinking mode 使 traces 更确定性（类似 QwQ-32B），C0 极低→SICA≡SC；(b) thinking mode 提升 constraint extraction 质量，打破 capability trap。预期 (a) 更可能。\", \"parent\": \"sica_expanded_14b\", \"conclusion\": \"killed\", \"notes\": \"Killed per D049: extraction_rate 73% below 80% threshold. Addresses W4 reviewer concern about reproducibility.（准备中）\\n- exp-013-constraint-ablation: MaxSAT satisfiability checking contributes minimal accuracy gains over naive constraint counting, because extracted constraints are too homogeneous (trace co-sourcing) to provide discriminative satisfiability signals. If SICA-MaxSAT ≈ SICA-count-only, the SAT solver is not the bottleneck — constraint quality is.（准备中）\\n- exp-d135-frontier-scb: 2026 frontier closed-source models (Claude/GPT/Gemini via OpenRouter) also exhibit SCB: self-extracted constraints will show BR≥2× with SICA≈SC, extending the scale-invariance finding from open-source 7B-72B to frontier closed-source models.（准备中）\\n- exp-d126-r1-distill-sica: Reasoning-distilled model (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-8B) does not escape SCB: self-extracted constraints will show BR≥2× and SICA≈SC, demonstrating that SCB is a structural property of autoregressive self-extraction that reasoning training cannot eliminate.（准备中）\\n- exp-d134-e6-closed-model: Closed-source frontier models (GPT-4o, Claude, etc.) also exhibit symbolic confirmation bias when self-extracting FOL constraints. If SCB persists at frontier scale with different architectures, this strengthens the claim that SCB is a fundamental property of self-verification rather than a scale or architecture artifact.（准备中）\\n- exp-d134-cil: CIL (Cumulative Information Lift) unifies combo gain prediction: CIL>0 iff verifier adds independent signal beyond generator. Cross-architecture NLI pairs show CIL>0 (predicting significant combo gain), while same-model SICA shows CIL≈0 (predicting no gain), validating CIL as the mechanistic criterion for escape.（准备中）\\n- exp-d134-baf-decoupled: Truncating CoT traces before the answer sentence reduces SCB: truncated-trace BR < full-trace BR, because answer leakage into FOL constraints is the primary SCB mechanism (Dir-H provenance showed late-step leakage 27.23% >> early 22.00%).（准备中）\\n- …另有 5 项\\n\\n## 近期结论\\n- **exp-folio-2x2-crossmodel** (success): Cross-model extraction on FOLIO-204 replicates ProofWriter 2×2 finding: extractor capability dominates SICA accuracy regardless of generator, and BR>1 persists in all cells. If confirmed, this generalizes the SCB universality + extractor dominance finding across two distinct reasoning datasets. ← 基于 exp-d126-crossmodel-sica\\n- **exp-d126-70b-sica** (failed): Symbolic confirmation bias persists at 72B scale: Qwen2.5-72B self-extracted constraints will show BR > 1 and κ ≥ 0.40, with SICA ≈ SC (no significant improvement), demonstrating that the independence bottleneck is scale-invariant beyond the 7B-14B range tested so far.\\n- **exp-d126-crossmodel-sica** (success): Cross-model constraint extraction (Generator: Qwen3-14B, Extractor: Mistral-7B, and vice versa) reduces symbolic confirmation bias compared to same-model extraction. If SCB persists at frontier scale with different architectures, this strengthens the claim that SCB is a fundamental property of self-verification rather than a scale or architecture artifact.（准备中）\\n- exp-d134-cil: CIL (Cumulative Information Lift) unifies combo gain prediction: CIL>0 iff verifier adds independent signal beyond generator. Cross-architecture NLI pairs show CIL>0 (predicting significant combo gain), while same-model SICA shows CIL≈0 (predicting no gain), validating CIL as the mechanistic criterion for escape.（准备中）\\n- exp-d134-baf-decoupled: Truncating CoT traces before the answer sentence reduces SCB: truncated-trace BR < full-trace BR, because answer leakage into FOL constraints is the primary SCB mechanism (Dir-H provenance showed late-step leakage 27.23% >> early 22.00%).（准备中）\\n- …另有 5 项\\n\\n## 近期结论\\n- **exp-folio-2x2-crossmodel** (success): Cross-model extraction on FOLIO-204 replicates ProofWriter 2×2 finding: extractor capability dominates SICA accuracy regardless of generator, and BR>1 persists in all cells. If confirmed, this generalizes the SCB universality + extractor dominance finding across two distinct reasoning datasets. ← 基于 exp-d126-crossmodel-sica\\n- **exp-d126-70b-sica** (failed): Symbolic confirmation bias persists at 72B scale: Qwen2.5-72B self-extracted constraints will show BR > 1 and κ ≥ 0.40, with SICA ≈ SC (no significant improvement), demonstrating that the independence bottleneck is scale-invariant beyond the 7B-14B range tested so far.\\n- **exp-d126-crossmodel-sica** (success): Cross-model constraint extraction (Generator: Qwen3-14B, Extractor: Mistral-7B, and vice versa) reduces symbolic confirmation bias compared to same-model extraction. If SCB stems from same-model distributional overlap, cross-model extraction should lower BR and improve SICA accuracy on ProofWriter N=204.\\n\\n## 趋势\\n成功 41 / 负向 76 / 失败 19 / 共 148\\n\\n## 下一步\\n（无活跃 idea，请通过 ideas.md 添加）\\n\\n## 资源状态\\nwestd-16639: 空闲 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3\\nwestd-25068: 无 GPU 信息\\n\\n## 告警\\n- mvp_math50_t07: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- sica_aime30_t07: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- sica_aime30_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- sica_aime30_v3: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- sica_proofwriter30: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- sica_folio30: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- sica_proofwriter30_v2: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-011-llama8b: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-010-mistral: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-011-llama8b-v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-017-strategyqa: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-018-arc: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-010-mistral-v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-017-strategyqa-v2: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- pilot-contrastive-prompt: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-028-qwen3-14b-thinking: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-033-qwen3-pw600-nonthinking: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-007-qwen72b-awq: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-004-qwen7b-rerun: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-042-premise-formalization: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-044-icl-oracle: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-042b-premise-formal: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-045b-backward-verify: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-028b-qwen3-thinking-folio204: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-049-mistral-logiqa200: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-050-mistral-icl-oracle-folio199: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-052-mistral-folio204-seed123: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-053-mistral-folio204-seed456: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-064: 已被终止 — unknown\\n- exp-075-contrastive-mistral-folio: 训练失败 — unknown\\n- exp-d126-70b-sica: 训练失败 — unknown\\n\", \"total_chars\": 6329, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"

_source line 44 · 2026-05-22T11:27:18.326Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01RK2tUA536smzmFE1Ru2xsi`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	name: on-experiment-failed
3	description: 实验失败后的自动诊断工作流——查日志分析原因、更新 registry、通知 Director。[CRON] 实验失败消息到达时使用。
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	- workflow
7	- diagnosis
8	- cron-hook
9	tools:
10	- read_registry
11	- tail_training_logs
12	- update_registry
13	- append_idea
14	- feishu_send_result_card
15	- spawn_worker
16	- check_job_status
17	version: 4
18	heavy: true
19	roles:
20	- agent
21	---
22	
23	# 实验失败诊断工作流
24	
25	## 触发方式
26	由 cron hook 自动触发：进程退出且 W&B 无数据 / W&B state=failed/crashed / NaN 检测到。
27	
28	## 步骤
29	
30	### Step 0: 读取项目上下文（诊断前必须执行）
31	先读文档，再看日志——否则诊断方向可能完全错误：
32	
33	- `read_project_file("memory.md")` — **最重要**：查是否有相同失败模式的历史记录和已知修复方案
34	- Worker 通过 `list_repo_tree` + `read_repo_file` 按需从代码仓库读取训练脚本结构
35	
36	读完后检查：
37	- memory.md 里有没有"此类实验曾失败，原因是 X"的记录？→ 直接按已知方案诊断，跳过 Step 2 通用流程
38	- 代码仓库中的训练入口和参数格式是否与本次实验配置匹配？（Worker 可用 `list_repo_tree` + `read_repo_file` 查看）
39	
40	### Step 1: 了解失败背景
41	- read_registry(exp_id) — 查看状态、conclusion、notes、metrics、log_path
42	
43	### Step 2: 浅层日志诊断
44	有 log_path 时：tail_training_logs(server, **绝对路径**, lines=80)
45	- 寻找：OOM、CUDA error、NaN、assertion error、数据加载错误、Python Traceback
46	- 没找到 → 增加行数再查
47	
48	### Step 2.5: 深查（满足以下任一条件时 spawn_worker）
49	- 运行时长 < 5 分钟（初始化阶段崩溃）
50	- tail 日志中未见明确 Traceback 或错误行
51	- 同类实验连续失败 ≥ 2 次
52	
53	```python
54	spawn_worker(
55	    task_type="diagnose:{exp_id}",
56	    task_prompt="""SSH 到服务器 {server}，诊断实验 {exp_id} 的失败原因。
57	
58	项目路径：{project_path}（绝对路径）
59	日志路径：{log_path}（可能为空）
60	
61	执行步骤（按顺序，有结论就停）：
62	
63	1. 如果有日志路径：
64	   - 先看结尾 50 行（找 Traceback / Error / CUDA OOM）
65	   - 没找到明确错误：再看开头 100 行（初始化崩溃在这里）
66	   - 日志文件不存在：说明任务启动即失败，跳到步骤 2
67	
68	2. 日志无错误或无日志时，检查基础设施：
69	   - 入口文件是否存在
70	   - 激活环境并验证 import
71	   - 检查 configs/ 目录
72	
73	3. 检查磁盘/内存（OOM 类）
74	
75	最后输出一行 JSON：
76	{"root_cause":"<引用具体报错行>","error_type":"infrastructure|hyperparameter|resource|data","fix_steps":["步骤1","步骤2"],"can_auto_fix":true}""",
77	    tool_groups=["ssh"],
78	)
79	```
80	
81	Worker 返回后，以其结论作为 Step 3 的诊断依据。
82	
83	### Step 3: 诊断 + 行动
84	根据 `error_type` 决定：
85	- **infrastructure**（文件缺失/import error/配置语法）→ spawn_worker 修复，修复后重提交（自主修复最多 1 次，再失败则停止告知用户）。注意：此重试策略仅适用于训练实验，数据生成任务的重试策略参见 `on-datagen-done` 的 Step 9。
86	  - spawn_worker 后立即：`update_registry(exp_id, "notes", "fix_worker_id: {worker_id}")`，确保关联可追溯
87	- **hyperparameter/架构**（loss 不降、指标差）→ 给具体调整建议，用户决定
88	- **resource**（OOM、GPU 不够）→ 给配置调整建议，用户确认
89	- **data**（路径、格式）→ spawn_worker 检查，能自修则修，不能则告知用户
90	
91	### Step 4: 更新 registry
92	- update_registry(exp_id, "notes", "失败原因: ... | 已有成果: ... | 建议: ...")
93	- update_registry(exp_id, "conclusion", "failed")
94	- update_registry(exp_id, "status", "DONE")
95	
96	### Step 5: 发送飞书结果卡片
97	- feishu_send_result_card(exp_id, conclusion="failed", key_metrics, baseline_comparison)
98	  - key_metrics：从 registry 取已有 metrics，没有则传空 dict
99	  - baseline_comparison：Step 3 的诊断摘要
100	
101	### Step 6: 提炼 insight + 更新文档
102	
103	**写入 memory.md（新失败模式才写，避免重复）**：
104	如果 root_cause 是之前未记录的模式（Step 0 读 memory.md 时没有看到），用 `save_memory` 追加：
105	```
106	- [失败模式] {error_type}：{root_cause 一句话摘要} → 修复方案：{fix_steps 摘要}
107	```
108	例：`- [失败模式] GPU 硬件：GPU 7 CUDA capability 检查失败，device=7, num_gpus=7 → 设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 跳过 GPU 7`
109	这样下次同类实验失败时，Step 0 读 memory.md 就能直接命中，跳过通用诊断流程。
110	
111	**更新 ideas.md（有价值的 insight 才写）**：
112	- AMP 崩溃反复出现 → `append_idea` 记录调参方向

      "content": "---\nname: on-experiment-failed\ndescription: 实验失败后的自动诊断工作流——查日志分析原因、更新 registry、通知 Director。[CRON] 实验失败消息到达时使用。\nscope: global\ntags:\n- workflow\n- diagnosis\n- cron-hook\ntools:\n- read_registry\n- tail_training_logs\n- update_registry\n- append_idea\n- feishu_send_result_card\n- spawn_worker\n- check_job_status\nversion: 4\nheavy: true\nroles:\n- agent\n---\n\n# 实验失败诊断工作流\n\n## 触发方式\n由 cron hook 自动触发：进程退出且 W&B 无数据 / W&B state=failed/crashed / NaN 检测到。\n\n## 步骤\n\n### Step 0: 读取项目上下文（诊断前必须执行）\n先读文档，再看日志——否则诊断方向可能完全错误：\n\n- `read_project_file(\"memory.md\")` — **最重要**：查是否有相同失败模式的历史记录和已知修复方案\n- Worker 通过 `list_repo_tree` + `read_repo_file` 按需从代码仓库读取训练脚本结构\n\n读完后检查：\n- memory.md 里有没有\"此类实验曾失败，原因是 X\"的记录？→ 直接按已知方案诊断，跳过 Step 2 通用流程\n- 代码仓库中的训练入口和参数格式是否与本次实验配置匹配？（Worker 可用 `list_repo_tree` + `read_repo_file` 查看）\n\n### Step 1: 了解失败背景\n- read_registry(exp_id) — 查看状态、conclusion、notes、metrics、log_path\n\n### Step 2: 浅层日志诊断\n有 log_path 时：tail_training_logs(server, **绝对路径**, lines=80)\n- 寻找：OOM、CUDA error、NaN、assertion error、数据加载错误、Python Traceback\n- 没找到 → 增加行数再查\n\n### Step 2.5: 深查（满足以下任一条件时 spawn_worker）\n- 运行时长 < 5 分钟（初始化阶段崩溃）\n- tail 日志中未见明确 Traceback 或错误行\n- 同类实验连续失败 ≥ 2 次\n\n```python\nspawn_worker(\n    task_type=\"diagnose:{exp_id}\",\n    task_prompt=\"\"\"SSH 到服务器 {server}，诊断实验 {exp_id} 的失败原因。\n\n项目路径：{project_path}（绝对路径）\n日志路径：{log_path}（可能为空）\n\n执行步骤（按顺序，有结论就停）：\n\n1. 如果有日志路径：\n   - 先看结尾 50 行（找 Traceback / Error / CUDA OOM）\n   - 没找到明确错误：再看开头 100 行（初始化崩溃在这里）\n   - 日志文件不存在：说明任务启动即失败，跳到步骤 2\n\n2. 日志无错误或无日志时，检查基础设施：\n   - 入口文件是否存在\n   - 激活环境并验证 import\n   - 检查 configs/ 目录\n\n3. 检查磁盘/内存（OOM 类）\n\n最后输出一行 JSON：\n{\"root_cause\":\"<引用具体报错行>\",\"error_type\":\"infrastructure|hyperparameter|resource|data\",\"fix_steps\":[\"步骤1\",\"步骤2\"],\"can_auto_fix\":true}\"\"\",\n    tool_groups=[\"ssh\"],\n)\n```\n\nWorker 返回后，以其结论作为 Step 3 的诊断依据。\n\n### Step 3: 诊断 + 行动\n根据 `error_type` 决定：\n- **infrastructure**（文件缺失/import error/配置语法）→ spawn_worker 修复，修复后重提交（自主修复最多 1 次，再失败则停止告知用户）。注意：此重试策略仅适用于训练实验，数据生成任务的重试策略参见 `on-datagen-done` 的 Step 9。\n  - spawn_worker 后立即：`update_registry(exp_id, \"notes\", \"fix_worker_id: {worker_id}\")`，确保关联可追溯\n- **hyperparameter/架构**（loss 不降、指标差）→ 给具体调整建议，用户决定\n- **resource**（OOM、GPU 不够）→ 给配置调整建议，用户确认\n- **data**（路径、格式）→ spawn_worker 检查，能自修则修，不能则告知用户\n\n### Step 4: 更新 registry\n- update_registry(exp_id, \"notes\", \"失败原因: ... | 已有成果: ... | 建议: ...\")\n- update_registry(exp_id, \"conclusion\", \"failed\")\n- update_registry(exp_id, \"status\", \"DONE\")\n\n### Step 5: 发送飞书结果卡片\n- feishu_send_result_card(exp_id, conclusion=\"failed\", key_metrics, baseline_comparison)\n  - key_metrics：从 registry 取已有 metrics，没有则传空 dict\n  - baseline_comparison：Step 3 的诊断摘要\n\n### Step 6: 提炼 insight + 更新文档\n\n**写入 memory.md（新失败模式才写，避免重复）**：\n如果 root_cause 是之前未记录的模式（Step 0 读 memory.md 时没有看到），用 `save_memory` 追加：\n```\n- [失败模式] {error_type}：{root_cause 一句话摘要} → 修复方案：{fix_steps 摘要}\n```\n例：`- [失败模式] GPU 硬件：GPU 7 CUDA capability 检查失败，device=7, num_gpus=7 → 设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 跳过 GPU 7`\n这样下次同类实验失败时，Step 0 读 memory.md 就能直接命中，跳过通用诊断流程。\n\n**更新 ideas.md（有价值的 insight 才写）**：\n- AMP 崩溃反复出现 → `append_idea` 记录调参方向\n- 部分 metrics 有价值但后期崩溃 → `append_idea` 记录调优方向\n\n更新 ideas.md IN_PROGRESS → DEAD ENDS：\n- move_idea(from_section=\"IN_PROGRESS（进行中）\", to_section=\"DEAD ENDS（已确认死路）\", index=N)\n- 找不到对应条目：跳过\n\n### Step 6.5: 更新 report.md\n\nreport.md 由系统根据 registry 状态自动生成，无需手动调用 `save_project_file`。Step 4 更新 registry 后，report 会自动刷新并在 ## 告警 区块突出标注本次失败。\n\n### Step 7: 回复\n生成失败报告：exp_id、失败原因（引用具体错误行）、已有成果、具体建议。\n",

(exp-031) delta=+14.22pp 证明上限存在，但 LLM extraction 质量是瓶颈\\n\\n### D080 全方向探索（2026-05-20 完成，12方向 8 negative / 3 positive-informational）\\n\\n**攻击 independence bottleneck 的全部路径均失败：**\\n\\n| 方向 | 类别 | 结果 | 核心发现 |\\n|------|------|------|----------|\\n| Wild1 MAX-UNSAT | 聚合规则 | ❌ | 排除约束太少(1.3/题), MIN-UNSAT Δ=-6.5pp |\\n| Wild6 κ-aware SC | 投票加权 | ❌ | accuracy与agreement单调递增, SC已最优 |\\n| Wild2 ASCII pad | 表面扰动 | ❌ | κ不变, 模型偏差非表面驱动 |\\n| Wild3 Perturbation | 语义扰动 | ❌ | κ反而上升, 变量重命名无效 |\\n| Wild4 Process-κ | 过程分析 | ❌ | 过程cos=0.69, 与答案不相关(r=-0.06, p=0.36) |\\n| DirA PROVE | 代码验证 | ❌ | 代码生成率15.2%, 结论性准确率50%(随机) |\\n| DirB DeepConf | 置信过滤 | ❌ | logprob与正确性无关(-0.135 vs -0.141) |\\n| DirD CEGIS | 迭代约束 | ❌ | R1 +2.4pp后饱和, 无迭代改善 |\\n| DirE Abstention | 选择性弃权 | ⚡ | AUROC=0.673, 80%cov +4.4pp |\\n| Cross-model κ | κ降低 | ✅ | κ_cross=0.422<κ_within=0.532, 但无accuracy增益 |\\n| Confirmation Bias | 因果分析 | ✅ | bias ratio 4.97x, 83.3%约束指向错误答案 |\\n\\n**关键教训：**\\n- **Logprob 不是质量信号**: Mistral-7B 对正确/错误答案同样过度自信, logprob 分布极度压缩(median=-0.06)\\n- **PROVE 需要强模型**: 7B 级模型无法为自然语言逻辑问题生成有效 verify() 函数, 这是能力门槛问题\\n- **CEGIS 单轮就饱和**: counterexample 引导的迭代优化在第一轮后即无新信息, 因为模型在 R1 就倾向于生成最容易的修补\\n- **Process similarity ≠ answer agreement**: 推理文本 cosine=0.69 高度相似但与答案一致性无统计相关, 说明 traces 在\\\"表面\\\"雷同但在推理关键步骤上分歧\\n- **Confirmation Bias 是因果解释**: 4.97x bias ratio 量化了为什么 self-extracted constraints 无法纠正多数票——它们本身就是多数票的产物\\n\\n### Wave 3 补充结果（2026-05-20）\\n\\n| 方向 | 结果 | 核心发现 |\\n|------|------|----------|\\n| I (Z3 Filter) | ❌ | filter precision 49.12%，Z3 SAT/UNSAT 无判别力 |\\n| J (Bootstrap Gold) | ❌ | R1 +2.45pp 后振荡，迭代无改善 |\\n| K (Minority Report) | ❌ | consistency precision 35.53%，少数派更 consistent ≠ 更正确 |\\n| F (Blind FOL) | 运行中 | 53/204, acc 39.6%, 75% 偏向 Unknown |\\n| G (Decoupling) | 运行中 | Intersection (NLI+SC agree): 68.48% (92/204 agree)\\n- **互补性**: NLI False 71.4% vs SC 38.1% (+33pp); SC Unknown 68.1% vs NLI 18.8% (+49pp)\\n- D088: Case A 确认, 论文切 V2 framing (cross-architecture verification)\\n\\n### 方法线完整矩阵（2026-05-20 全部完成，12/12 cells ALL negative）\\n\\n| Method | FOLIO s42 | FOLIO s123 | LogiQA s42 | LogiQA s123 | mean Δ |\\n|--------|-----------|------------|------------|-------------|--------|\\n| ICG | -1.96pp | +1.47pp | -0.5pp | -3.5pp | -1.1pp |\\n| Contrastive | -9.31pp | -5.39pp | -4.5pp | -2.5pp | -5.4pp |\\n| Debate | -18.63pp | -15.20pp | -23.0pp | -21.5pp | -19.6pp |\\n\\n**战略结论**：prompt-level 9 种 + method-level 3 种 + D080 8方向全败 = independence bottleneck 不可通过同模型方案解决。方法失败本身是论文贡献。\\n\\n**方法线严重度排序**：Debate >> Contrastive >> ICG ≈ neutral。Debate 在所有条件下 catastrophic negative（可能 debate 退化推理链而非增强独立性），Contrastive 中度 negative（~1/4 题 degenerate 为 SC），ICG 接近零（模型偏差从问题理解阶段就存在）。\\n\\n### 分析产出汇总（2026-05-19~20）\\n- **McNemar 统计检验**: 6/6 method×dataset pairs, 5/6 n.s., exp-076 FOLIO p=0.013 (Debate significantly worse)\\n- **约束消融 (exp-013)**: MaxSAT ≈ count-only ≈ random-weight → SAT solver 无贡献，约束质量太低\\n- **Error categorization**: Debate vs Contrastive discordant Jaccard=0.19（不同失败模式）\\n- **Constraint quality**: SICA redundancy 42.9%, Contrastive trivially_sat 62.7%\\n- **κ 测量**: ICG κ=0.465 (Plan B), Diverse-Prompt κ=0.403, SC κ=0.438 → same-model ceiling κ≈0.40-0.47\\n- **Scatter plot (f6)**: κ-accuracy 4点图，展示 Debate 低κ高代价、其余三方法 same-model ceiling\\n\\n### ICG 3-seed 结论（2026-05-19）\\n- ICG (Independent Constraint Generation) 绕过 trace-sourced 约束，直接从 premises 独立提取\\n- 3-seed Mistral-7B FOLIO-204: Δ=-1.96/+1.47/-0.98pp, mean=-0.49pp±1.77pp\\n- **机制洞察**: bottleneck 不仅是\\\"约束来自 traces\\\"——模型推理偏差从问题理解阶段就存在，独立生成的约束继承相同的 model bias\\n\\n### Fleiss' κ / Effective K 完整数据（2026-05-19）\\n\\n**公式：Eff-K = K / (1 + (K-1) × κ)**\\n\\n| Exp | Model | Dataset | κ | Eff-K | SICA Δpp | p |\\n|-----|-------|---------|---|-------|----------|---|\\n| exp-033 | Mistral-7B | FOLIO-204 s42 | 0.5006 | **1.84** | +4.90 | 0.013 |\\n| **3.62** | -2.83 | 0.010 |\\n| exp-026/041 | Qwen-14B | FOLIO-204 | 0.7440 | **1.31** | +0.25 | n.s. |\\n| exp-038 | Qwen-14B | LogiQA-200 | 0.8210 | **1.20** | -0.50 | n.s. |\\n\\n### Same-model κ ceiling（2026-05-20）\\n- ICG κ=0.465 (Plan B per-constraint-set independent MAX-SAT raters)\\n- Diverse-Prompt κ=0.403 (exp-077)\\n- SC κ=0.438 (exp-077)\\n- Debate κ=0.155 (exp-077, 但 accuracy 38.24% → trade independence for accuracy)\\n- **结论**: 除 Debate 外，所有 same-model 方法收敛到 κ≈0.40-0.47，无论如何设计独立性策略\\n\\n### Seed Replication 结论（2026-05-19）\\n- exp-033 (seed=42): delta=+4.90pp, p=0.013 ✅\\n- exp-052 (seed=123): delta=+3.43pp, p=0.039 ✅\\n- exp-053 (seed=456): delta=-0.49pp, p=1.0 ❌\\n- 3 seeds 中 2 positive 1 negative，Pearson r(SC%, delta)=-0.8973\\n\\n### exp033 Seed 审计（2026-05-20）\\n- **exp033 实际 seed=None**（代码 default=None, 日志无 seed 记录）\\n- `random.seed()` 仅控制 Python 内部随机（问题排序），**不影响 vLLM 采样**\\n- SC=54.41% 与 seed=789 (exp067) 精确匹配（均 111/204），可能巧合\\n- seed=42 对应 SC=53.92% (exp074), seed=123→57.35%, seed=456→55.88%\\n\\n### 计算成本数据（2026-05-20）\\n- SICA 总开销 = SC 的 **4.5x**（81.4 min vs ~17.7 min, 204题 K=12）\\n- 分阶段: trace generation 5.2s/q (22%) | constraint extraction 对正确/错误答案同样过度自信, logprob 分布极度压缩(median=-0.06)\\n- **PROVE 需要强模型**: 7B 级模型无法为自然语言逻辑问题生成有效 verify() 函数, 这是能力门槛问题\\n- **CEGIS 单轮就饱和**: counterexample 引导的迭代优化在第一轮后即无新信息, 因为模型在 R1 就倾向于生成最容易的修补\\n- **Process similarity ≠ answer agreement**: 推理文本 cosine=0.69 高度相似但与答案一致性无统计相关, 说明 traces 在\\\"表面\\\"雷同但在推理关键步骤上分歧\\n- **Confirmation Bias 是因果解释**: 4.97x bias ratio 量化了为什么 self-extracted constraints {
  "task_type": "diagnose:exp-multi-seed-mistral-folio",
  "project": "symb_invariant_consensus",
  "task_prompt": "SSH 到服务器 westd-16639，诊断实验 exp-multi-seed-mistral-folio 的失败原因。\n\n## 背景\n- 实验：Multi-seed replication of Mistral-7B × FOLIO-204, seed=789（123/456 已有）\n- PID: 576938, cuda:3, port 8004\n- Cron 报告：\"Process exited without valid W&B metrics\"\n- 日志路径: /tmp/multiseed_mistral_folio_cuda3_nohup.log\n\n## 重要历史模式\nexp-070 曾出现 EXIT_CODE=129 (SIGHUP) 但日志显示全部完成的误报。本实验可能是同样情况。\n\n## 诊断步骤\n\n1. 先检查日志是否存在，读取尾部 80 行，看有没有 Traceback / Error / CUDA OOM / 或者正常完成的 DONE 标记\n2. 如果日志显示正常完成（比如有 \"DONE\" 或 \"Completed\" 或所有题都跑完），这是 cron 误报，提取实际结果指标\n3. 如果日志有错误，读取更多上下文（开头 100 行 + 错误前后 50 行）定位 root cause\n4. 检查进程是否仍在运行: `ps -p 576938 -o pid,stat,etime 2>/dev/null`\n5. 检查 cuda:3 上有没有残留 vLLM 进程: `nvidia-smi -i 3`\n\n## 输出格式\n最后输出一行 JSON：\n{\"root_cause\":\"<引用具体报错行或完成标记>\",\"error_type\":\"infrastructure|hyperparameter|resource|data|false_alarm\",\"actual_complete\":true/false,\"metrics\":{\"sc_acc\":...,\"sica_acc\":...,\"delta\":...},\"fix_steps\":[\"步骤1\",\"步骤2\"],\"can_auto_fix\":true/false}\n\n如果是 false_alarm（实际完成），metrics 字段必须从日志中提取实际的 SC accuracy、SICA accuracy、delta 等指标。",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ],
  "context": "服务器环境: westd-16639, 4× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell, conda base (/root/miniconda3)。项目目录: /root/symb_invariant_consensus。vLLM 需要: VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER=0, --enforce-eager。"
}
